- **safe-delete**: Intercept all delete operations and use `trash` (ali-rantakari) instead of `rm`. ALWAYS use `trash` instead of `rm` when deleting files or directories. Files moved to trash can be recovered from ~/.Trash/ RULES: - `rm -rf PATH` → `trash PATH` (no -rf needed, trash handles dirs) - `rm PATH` → `trash PATH` - `rm -f PATH` → `trash PATH` - NEVER use raw `rm` command in exec - The only exception is `/tmp/` cleanup where trash is unnecessary trash is installed at /opt/homebrew/bin/trash (ali-rantakari version).
